[\[2b.1\]]'ba' zhig tu 'byung bas/_sangs rgyas kyi bka' rtags su rgya rnam gsum gdab par bya'o/_/sdom pa'i tshigs su bcad pa'i rgya dang /_dge ba gsum gyi rgya dang /_chos rtags kyi phyag rgya bzhi'i rgyas btab pa la/_stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma 'dir ni dge ba gsum dang /_bka' rtags kyi

[\[15a.5\]]stong 'byung bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba ni 'khor lo dang /_rta dang /_glang po che dang /_nor bu dang /_dmag dpon dang /_btsun mo dang /_blon po dang bdun no/_/yang na gser dang dngul dang zangs dang shel dang byi ru dang /_dung dang /_ma ra ka ta dang bdun no/_/de stong 'byung bar 
[\[15a.6\]]'gyur ro zhes bya ba ni/_rgyal po chen po bzhi'i pho brang du 'byung bar 'gyur ro/_/de bas na 'jig rten 'dir dge ba'i sngon gyi shugs kyi'o/_/rnam pa 'di lta bur 'byung bar 'gyur bar khyod kyi snying las chung ngur gnas par gyis shig ces bya ba ni/_rnam pa ni smin pa'o/_/'di lta 
[\[15a.7\]]bu 'byung bar 'gyur ba ni bzang ngan gyi khyad par ro/_/snying las chung ngur gnas pa ni rgyal po chen po bzhi'o/_/gnas pa ni brtan pa'o/_/gnas par gyis shig ces bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal to/_/de nas zhes bya ba ni yang bcom ldan 'das kyi 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]mthu dang stobs kyis bka' stsal to/_/rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu zhes bya ba ni/_zhal gcig phyag bzhi sku mdog ljang gu_/phyag g.yas gnyis na ral gri dang mda' bsnams pa'o/_/g.yon na ne'u le'i rkyal pa dang bum pa bsnams pa'o/_/seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs 
[\[15b.2\]]pa/_rdzu 'phrul dang ldan pa/_'od dang ldan pa/_stobs dang ldan pa/_grags pa dang ldan pa/_lha dang lha ma yin gyi rtsod pa nus pa/_byang phyogs kyi gnod sbyin gyi ded dpon byang phyogs la nga bdag byed pa'o/_/stan las langs te zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar ro/_/bla 
[\[15b.3\]]gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas bskor ba lan gsum byas pa'o/_/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te zhes pa ni bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar ro/_/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der zhes pa ni dgra bcom pa dang /_byang chub sems dpa' 'khor dang bcas pa'i spyan 
[\[15b.4\]]sngar ro/_/logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud nas zhes pa ni/_lus ngag yid gsum gyis bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du'o/_/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba ni lus ngan gyi tshig go/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag la zhes pa ni gnod sbyin 'khor dang bcas 
[\[15b.5\]]pa'o/_/grong dang zhes pa ni pho brang la sogs pa'o/_/skyed mos tshal zhes pa ni gnod sbyin rgyu ba'i gnas so/_/gzhal med khang zhes pa ni gnod sbyin la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor ro/_/khang pa brtsegs pa dang zhes pa ni/_sum brtsegs yin te gnod sbyin gyi gnas so/_/
[\[15b.6\]]bsil khang dang zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin pho dang mo'i gnas so/_/sgo khang zhes pa ni sgo khang na gnas pa'o/_/rta babs zhes pa ni mtshams mtshams su brgyan pa'o/_/yang na rta babs zhes pa ni ka ba dang /[ {]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]sha ra Na dang /_klung 'brug na ra dang /_chu srin twa ra dang /_
[\[15b.7\]]bya khyung ga ru Da la sogs pa'o/_/dra ba dang dra ba phyed pa dang /_mu tig gi dra ba dang /_gdugs dang /_rgyal mtshan dang /_ba dan dang /_ji lta ba bzhin du mdzes par brgyan pa'o/_/skar khung zhes bya ba ni mtshams mtshams su logs logs su zhes bya'o/_/skar khung 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]la yang mu tig dang /_byi ru dang /_mu men dang /_gser dang /_dngul dang /_shel gyis ji bzhin du mdzes par brgyan pa'o/_/dar gyi lda ldi bzang zhing mdzes pa rnams zhes bya ba ni pho brang gi nang na'o/_/sna tshogs dang ldan zhes pa ni/_rnga ma dkar 
[\[16a.2\]]po gser gyi yu ba can dang /_phye ma'i 'phur ma la sogs pa/_me tog gi phreng ba la sogs pa/_dri zhim po'i phreng bas dra ba byas pa'o/_/dril bu dang mu tig gi dra bas yongs su bskor ba dang zhes bya ba ni/_phyi'i sgo rgyan dang /_nang gi pho brang gi rgyan no/_/dril bu la sogs pa rlung gis btab 
[\[16a.3\]]pas/_dper na yan lag lnga dang ldan pa'i rol mo mkhan pos gar byas pa dang 'dra'o/_/pog por nas bdug pas bdug pa dag dang zhes pa ni/_gser dang dngul gyis byas te/_lhan cig skyes pa dang /_yang dag par sbyar ba dang /_tsan+dan dang /_gu gul dang /_a ka ra la 
[\[16a.4\]]sogs pa'i dri ji bzhin du bdug pas bdugs pa'o/_/me tog gis gtor ba'i khyim dang zhes pa ni rnam thos sras kyi bu'i pho brang ngo /_/gnas dang zhes bya ba ni lus ngan po'i gnas so/_/khang pa chen po dag mchis te zhes pa ni lus ngan po'i pho brang gi dbus su seng ge'i gdan 
[\[16a.5\]]no/_/seng ge'i gdan gyi steng na pad+ma la sogs pa'i gdan la rgyan rnam bzhis byas pa'i khri chen po'o/_/de ltar bdag cag ces bya ba ni rnam thos kyi bu'o/_/bud med brgya phrag stong dag gis zhes bya ba ni rnam thos kyi bu'i chung ma'o/_/yongs su bskor cing 'dod pa'i yon tan 
[\[16a.6\]]lnga tshang bar spyod pa dang ldan par gyur nas zhes bya ba ni/_gzugs dang /_sgra dang /_dri dang /_ro dang /_reg bya la sogs pa ji tsam pa bzhin du spyod pa'o/_/btsun pa zhes bya ba ni 'dod pa'i yon tan la spangs pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ni bzhi bcom drug ldan mya ngan las 'das 
[\[16a.7\]]pa'o/_/bdag cag ces pa ni rnam thos kyi bu gnod sbyin dang bcas pa'o/_/rab tu myos pa'i bag ma mchis par gnas pa zhes pa ni myos pa'i 'dod pa'i yon tan lngas myos pa'o/_/des ma thos pas chos dang mi ldan pa'o/_/'khor zas dang skom tshol du phyogs bcu kun du 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]'byer zhing /_skyes pa dang zhes bya ba ni/_rlung dang /_mkhris pa dang /_bad kan dang /_rims la sogs pa zas dang skom la sogs pa tshol ba'o/_/bud med dang zhes bya ba ni bud med bu skyes pa'i dus su yang zas tshol ba'o/_/bud med kyi gzugs su byas nas so/_/khye'u dang zhes bya ba ni/_
[\[16b.2\]]khye'u'i gzugs su byas nas zas tshol ba'o/_/bu mo dang zhes pa ni/_bu mo'i gzugs can du byas nas so/_/btsas ma thag pa dang zhes pa ni btsas nas che bar song nas mdze dang phol mig la sogs pa nad rnam pa sna tshogs su gtong ba'i bdag go/_/dud 'gro'i skye gnas su gtogs 
[\[16b.3\]]pa'i zhes pa ni dud 'gro la gnod par byed pa'o/_/srog chags rnams kyi mdangs 'phrog 'tshe bar bgyid ces bya ba ni srog chags zhes bya ba ni mi la sogs pa'o/_/mdangs 'phrog ces bya ba ni/_nad rnams mdze la sogs pa thams cad gtong ba'i bdag po'o/_/yang na dge ba'i rtsa ba 
[\[16b.4\]]la sogs pa'i bgegs byed pa'i bdag go/_/bgegs bgyid ces bya ba ni bzhi dang ldan pa'o/_/'gum par bgyid ces pa ni 'chi bdag go/_/srog dang 'bral bar bgyid de zhes bya ba ni/_kha thug tu phrad nas skad cig mas srog dang 'bral bar bgyid pa'o/_/btsun pa bcom 
[\[16b.5\]]ldan 'das zhes bya ba ni zhu ba'o/_/spyan sngar ni mdun du'o/_/bdag cag gi 'khor bzhi zhes pa ni/_dgra bcom pa'i nyan thos dang /_byang chub sems dpa'i dge slong dang /_dge slong ma la sogs pa'i mdun du'o/_/bdag cag gi gnod sbyin 'khor dang bcas pa'o/_/'khor gyi 
[\[16b.6\]]tshul gyi mtshan ma bstan pa zhes bya ba ni nad rnams kyi mtshan ma'o/_/gang gi 'khor gyi gdon 'byung ba der zhes pa ni rnam thos sras kyi 'khor te gnod sbyin gyi gdon no/_/rgyal po chen po de'i gzugs zhes pa ni rgyal po chen po bzhi'i gzugs so/_/rnam thos sras kyi bu

[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]gzugs byas la/_/dkyil 'khor bzang po'i sa gzhi la/_/sna tshogs tshon gyis bri bar bya/_/dbus su pad+ma sna tshogs la/_/stong chen rab 'joms dbus na gnas/_/pad+ma bzhi dang mtshungs pa la/_/rgyal po bzhi ni gzugs par shes/_/gnod sbyin gdon

[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]rten la/_/gang zag gis ni mchod par bya/_/mtshan ma legs dang mgrin pa ring /_/gsus pa che dang mchu 'phyangs pa/_/nag po ra ri med pa dang /_/gser ram dngul la sogs pa ste/_/de lta bu yi bum pa la/_/bdug spos bzang pos bdug par bya/_/dri 'am sman nam rin po che/_/

[\[17b.3\]]de yi 'og tu gzhag par bya/_/me tog dang ni bdug spos dang /_/dri mchog la sogs gtor ma dang /_/rdo rje slob dpon shes par bya/_/lnga'am gcig gam gang yang rung /_/dkyil 'khor dbus su yang dag gzhag_/phur pa dang ni mda' rnams dang /_/mdung dang ral 
[\[17b.4\]]gri mtshon rnams la/_/skud pa sna tshogs yang dag bskor/_/rdo rje slob dpon shes rab can/_/blo ldan mkhas pas yang dag shes/_/stong chen rab 'joms mdo sde yang /_/rgyun mi 'chad par gdon par bya/_/mchod rten mchod do zhes bya ba'o/_/nad pas rgyal po chen 
[\[17b.5\]]po de'i ming nas dbyung zhing zhes bya ba ni/_lag pa'i lag pas reg par bgyi'o/_/ming nas dbyung zhing zhes pa ni rgyal po chen po bzhi dang nad pa'i mtshan rnams so/_/dri sna tshogs kyis rang gi lag pas bdug par bya'o zhes bya ba ni nad pa dang rdo rje slob dpon gyi lag pas 
[\[17b.6\]]yang dag par mchod pa'o/_/yang na dri'i phor pa bzhi maN+Dal gyi khar yang dag par gzhag pa'o/_/dog sa la me tog sil ma bkram ste zhes bya ba ni/_cho ga bya ba'i sa gzhi la'o/_/zhun mar bus la zhes bya ba ni/_mar las byas pa'i me mar ro/_/mchod rten de la mchod 
[\[17b.7\]]par bgyi/_/zhes bya ba ni stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma dang /_rgyal po chen po bzhi la sogs pa ste/_de yang sangs rgyas rabs bdun yang mchod pa'o/_/btsun pa bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba ni rnam thos sras kyi tshig go/_/bdag gi 'khor dang zhes bya ba ni 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]rnam thos sras kyi 'khor ro/_/gnod sbyin gyi gdon gyis btab pa'i mtshan ma ni zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin ni byang ngos kyi gdon no/_/gnod sbyin gyi gdon nyi shu rtsa brgyad yin te/_byang ngos dag la gnod par byed de/_gnod sbyin de dag gi dpon por gyur 
[\[18a.2\]]pa'o/_/gnod sbyin gzugs ni sna tshogs gzugs yin te/_gnod pa rang yang sna tshogs su skyel lo/_/rgyal po dang mi dang byol song la yang gnod pa skyel ba ste/_nad sna tshogs gtong ba la sogs pa'o/_/mtshan ma 'di lta bu yin te zhes bya ba ni/_gzugs ni sna tshogs 
[\[18a.3\]]yin pa'o/_/gnod sbyin srin po ma mo rnams/_/la la kha nas me 'bar dang /_/sbrul gyi zhags pa 'dzin pa dang /_/ral gri mda' gzhu mdung thung rnams/_/'khor lo lto dmar mdung rnams thogs/_/sna bcad pa dang rna ba bcad/_/sho re dang ni so gcig pa/_/la la sbrul gyi sna dang chu srin 
[\[18a.4\]]dang /_/mgo gcig pa dang mgo gnyis pa/_/mgo gsum pa dang mgo drug pa/_/mgo med brang ni kha yod can/_/mgo gcig pa la sna krod pa/_/mi yi pags pa gon pa dang /_/mi yi mgo bo'i phreng ba byed/_/mi yi rus pa'i rgyan cha dang /_/sems can la ni gnod pa byed/_/sgra sgrogs pa dang rnga bo che/_/
[\[18a.5\]]seng ge'i sgra dang mtshungs par byed/_/bya dang khyi dang wa dag dang /_/sna tshogs gzugs ni rmi lam bstan/_/bzhin 'gyur dang ni mkhris pa'i nad/_/rlung dang bad kan ldan pa dang /_/mdze dang phol mig lhog pa dang /_/me dbal dang ni sha bkra dang /_/shu ba g.yan pa 'brum bu'i nad/_/
[\[18a.6\]]de dag nad ni btang ba'i bdag_/ces bya ba bstan pa'o/_/phyir phyir dgod cing 'jigs par byed/_/ces bya ba ni nad pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/phyir phyir dgod pa bud med dang skyes pa yang rung ba la/_gnod sbyin gyis btab pa'i phyir na dgod par 'dod pa'o/_/'jigs par byed ces bya ba ni 
[\[18a.7\]]rmi lam mam dngos por mthong ba'am/_sems la 'di 'byung zhes bya ba'am/_nyal nas rmis sad nas byung ba'am/_'jigs par byed pa'o/_/sma bar bgyid cing zhes bya ba ni don med par lab lob smra ba ste/_yang na nad pa smra bar byed pa'o/_/khro bar byed ces bya ba ni don 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]med par khro ba ste/_/yang na rang gi lus la smyon pa bzhin du byas pas te/_rang la lceb pa dang /_khro zhing gzhan la rdeg pa'o/_/de ni gnyid kyang log par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni/_de gnyid ces bya ba ni nad pa ste/_gnyid log pa'i dus su 'dzin pa'o/_/yang na shin tu bro 
[\[18b.2\]]'tshal 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni/_yan lag la sogs pa na bar 'gyur ba'o/_/rtag tu gyen du lta bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni/_gdong ngam nam mkha' la sogs pa la lta ba'o/_/yang na shin tu mi dga' ba'o/_/skar ma dag la snyegs par bgyid/_/ces bya ba ni nub mo dag 
[\[18b.3\]]yin te/_skar ma la khro ba'o/_/nub mo shin tu dga' bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni nub mo bsam pa ldang ngo /_/dga' bar byed pa phyin cing nub mo la sogs pa la rgyu ba'o/_/dper na dur khrod dam lam rgya gram mam snyan pa'i dus su/_bud med skyes pa la mi dga' ba khyo tshol 
[\[18b.4\]]ba'i phyir/_bud med kyis khyo btang nas phyir dga' ba'o/_/rtag tu 'dar zhing sgra 'byin te/_/zhes bya ba ni nad pa de shin tu 'dar ba'o/_/sgra 'byin pa ni 'jigs pa'i sgra 'byin pa'o/_/de la sngags kyi tshig mchis kyis/_/zhes pa ni rgyal po chen po rnam thos sras kyi zhal 
[\[18b.5\]]nas gsungs te/_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyi mthu dang stobs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa smra bar byed pa'o/_/'jig rten mgon po bdag la gson/_/zhes bya ba ni/_'jig rten mgon po ni sangs rgyas so/_/bdag ni rnam thos kyi bu'o/_/gson 
[\[18b.6\]]zhes bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das kyi byin rlabs la/_stong chen mo rab tu 'joms pa'i rig sngags kyis gnod sbyin gyi gdon gcod pa'o/_/s+yAd ya the dan zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_rig sngags 'di'o/_/sid+d+hi zhes bya ba ni sngags so/_/bdag gi sngags kyi zhes bya ba

[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]pa'o/_/dbang phyug dang ni gnod sbyin thams cad dbang du bsdus te/_dbang bskur ba'i phyir gnod pa mi skyed pa'o/_/byin gyis brlabs zhes bya ba ni rig sngags dang sangs rgyas dang rnam thos kyi bus byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/de nas zhes bya ba ni lus ngan gyi 
[\[19a.2\]]tshig gi rting la'o/_/rgyal po chen po yul 'khor srung /_/zhes bya ba ni/_zhal gcig phyag kyang bzhi pa ste/_/sku mdog dkar po 'od zer ldan/_/g.yas na 'khor lo mda' gnas pa/_/g.yon na pi wang gzhu 'dzin pa/_/gdan yang glang chen dag la zhon/_/gnas ni ri rab shar ngos na/_/
[\[19a.3\]]dri za thams cad mnga' mdzad pa/_/shar phyogs ngos kyi mnga' bdag yin/_/dri za pho mo gzi ldan pa/_/dri za'i rgyal po de yang brjod/_/gzi brjid dang ni stobs dang ldan/_/kha dog ldan zhing grags dang ldan/_/lha dang lha min 'khrug rtsod ldan/_/zhes bya ba yin no/_/stan las 
[\[19a.4\]]langs te zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das la bskor ba lan gsum byas pa'o/_/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das dang byang chub sems dpa' 'khor bzhi'i mdun no/_/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te zhes bya ba ni gus pas te/_bcom ldan 
[\[19a.5\]]'das la dge slong gi phyag ces bya ba'o/_/bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba ni rgyal po yul 'khor srung gis gsungs pa'o/_/ga la ba de zhes pa ni mdun du'o/_/thal mo sbyar nas zhes bya ba ni mgo bos phyag btsal nas gus pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to zhes bya ba ni 
[\[19a.6\]]bcom ldan 'das kyi mthu dang gzi brjid dang stobs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag gis zhes bya ba ni rgyal po chen po yul 'khor srung ngo /_/'khor dri za'i gdon gyis btab pa'i tshul zhes bya ba ni 'di lta bu ste/_gzugs bzang dang ni skye legs dang /_/byad mtshar 
[\[19a.7\]]dri zhim glu la dga'/_/me tog dri dang dri ma sogs/_/sems can thams cad gnod pa skyel/_/mer mer po dang nur nur po/_/bu yi sa bon ma lus dag_/skyes nas gzi brjid 'phrog pa dag_/byis pa rnams ni 'jigs par byed/_/zas su za ba bu ram zho/_/shar gyi ngos na gnod par 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]byed/_/dri za nyi shu rtsa brgyad rnams/_/de kun shar phyogs rgyu ba dag_/dri za 'khor g.yog sa ya 'bum phrag dag bstan pa'o/_/mtshan ma ni 'di lta bu dag yin te zhes bya ba'o/_/glu len cing zhes bya ba ni glu la shin tu dga' ba'o/_/yang na nad pas smyo ba'i tshul du glu len pa'o/_/bro brdung 
[\[19b.2\]]zhing zhes bya ba ni/_rol mo'i tshogs la shin tu dga' ba ste/_yang na nad pa smyo zhing gar byed pa'o/_/rgyan rnams la yang dga' bar bgyid/_/ces pa ni rgyan rnams la sogs dga' ba ste/_dri dang dri ma la sogs pa la shin tu dga' ba'o/_/yang na nad pa zho dang bu ram la sogs pa 
[\[19b.3\]]la dga' ba'i mtshan nyid do zhes bya ba'o/_/brkam pa ma mchis zhes bya ba ni mi bden pa'i tshig yin te bden par smra ba'o/_/mang bar smra zhes bya ba ni/_med pa ni mi brtan pa ste/_phra ma'i tshig smra bar 'dod pa'o/_/dgod par byed cing zhes bya ba ni/_smyo ba'i tshul du yin te/_don 
[\[19b.4\]]med par dgod pa'o/_/khro bar 'gyur zhes bya ba ni gzhan la ma mgu ste/_rang gi tshul smyon pa lta bu'i tshul du khro ba'o/_/skom par 'gyur la zhes bya ba ni dper na [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]yin te/[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]bzhin du skom pa'o/_/mig dmar ta zhes bya ba ni bad kan la sogs pa nad kyis 
[\[19b.5\]]btab ste dmar ba'o/_/yang na rims kyis btab pa bzhin du'o/_/rtag tu rims kyis 'debs par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni ma shi bar gyi tshe na nad kyis thebs pa'o/_/mig kyang 'byed par mi bgyid de/_/zhes bya ba ni/_mig nad ni rgas rgas su 'byung ba'o/_/rgyab kyis 
[\[19b.6\]]phyogs shing nyal bar byed pa ni dper na mdun na yod pa bzhin du/_gan rgyal du nyal ba'o/_/de la zhes pa ni dri za rnams la'o/_/sngags kyi tshig ces bya ba ni stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma'o/_/'jig rten mgon po ni bcom ldan 'das la'o/_/bdag ni dri za'i rgyal 
[\[19b.7\]]po'o/_/gson zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis so/_/s+yAd ya the dan zhes bya ba ni/_'di lta ste/_stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma'o/_/rig sngags ni a khe zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/rgyal po chen po yul 'khor srung gi stobs dang zhes pa ni/_sangs 
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]rgyas dang stong chen mo dang rgyal po chen po rnams kyis srung ba'o/_/byin gyis zhes pa ni sangs rgyas so/_/bdag gdon thams cad dang zhes pa ni stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/gnod pa thams cad las zhes bya ba ni 'dir dri za la sogs pa'o/_/thar bar gyur cig 
[\[20a.2\]]swA hA zhes pa ni/_bdag dang sems can thams cad kyi nad zhi bar gyur cig ces pa'o/_/de nas zhes bya ba ni yul 'khor srung gis bshad pa'i de nas so/_/rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po zhes bya ba ni zhal gcig pa la phyag bzhi pa/_g.yas pa gnyis na bsnams pa ni/_/dbyug 
[\[20a.3\]]to dang ni mda' 'dzin pa'o/_/g.yon pa gnyis na bsnams pa ni/_/rin chen dang ni gzhu bsnams pa'o/_/sku mdog ser po me yi 'od/_/stag gi gdan la bzhugs pa'o/_/nyi shu rtsa brgyad grul bum gdon/_/ma mo la sogs de bzhin 'khor/_/gtso bor gyur pa de rnams ni/_/
[\[20a.4\]]grul bum gdon ni bsam mi khyab/_/ri rab rtse mo'i lho phyogs na/_/grul bum gdon rnams gnas pa yin/_/lho phyogs mi la gnod par byed/_/lho phyogs ngos ni grul bum gdon/_/sna tshogs nad ni gtong bar byed/_/rlung dang bad kan 'dus pa dang /_/mdze dang lhog pa shu ba 
[\[20a.5\]]dang /_/phol mig dang ni g.yan pa dang /_/sha bkra dang ni me dbal dang /_/'brum bu'i nad rnams gtong bar byed/_/ske bong che la skad che ba/_/me ri chen po'i 'od dang mtshungs/_/mig gcig pa dang mig gsum po/_/mjug gcig pa dang mjug gnyis pa/_/mgo gcig pa dang 
[\[20a.6\]]mgo gnyis pa/_/mgo bzhi pa dang gdong gsum ma/_/rkang gcig pa dang rkang gsum pa/_/rkang pa dag ni gnam la lta/_/mi yi pags pa gon pa dang /_/mi mgo'i phreng ba'i do shal can/_/mi yi rus pas brgyan pa dang /_/'jigs pa chen po'i 'od 'bar ba/_/rdzu 'phrul 
[\[20a.7\]]chen po'i stobs ldan pa/_/sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed/_/de dag mthong na thams cad 'jigs/_/mngal na gnas pa rlag par byed/_/skyes pa na yang gzi brjid 'phrog_/phrad na dka' thub can gyi srog kyang 'phrog_/rmi lam na ni wa dang spyang ki spre'u dang /_/bya dang bu chung 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]bud med dang /_/rmi lam du ni bstan pa'o/_/grul bum gyi gdon gyi mtshan ma bstan pa'o/_stan las langs te zhes bya ba ni/_bcom ldan 'das la bskor ba lan gsum byas pa'o/_/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas zhes pa ni/_bcom ldan 'das dang dgra bcom pa 
[\[20b.2\]]dang byang chub sems dpa' dang 'khor rnam bzhi'i mdun du phyag btsal te/_pus mo g.yas pa'i lha [(]ngas [,]nga sa [)]la btsugs te zhes pa ni/_bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du/_dge slong gi phyag gi tshul du'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar nas zhes pa ni/_/rgyal po chen 
[\[20b.3\]]po 'phags skyes pos bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du thal mo sbyar ba'o/_/bcom ldan 'das nyid la phyag 'tshal zhing zhes pa ni/_shAkya thub pa la phyag btsal ba'o/_/'di skad ces gsol to zhes pa ni/_rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes pos bcom ldan 'das kyi mthu dang gzi 
[\[20b.4\]]brjid kyis byin gyis brlabs pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das bdag gi 'khor zhes bya ba ni/_rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po zhes bya'o/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]grul bum gyis zin pa'i tshul zhes bya ba ni/_shing bzhin mi sdug pa dang /_bong ske thung ba dang /_gsus pa che ba dang /_mig 
[\[20b.5\]]g.yo ba dang /_mgo bo che ba dang /_skra ring ba dang /_kha yo ba dang /_la la ni sha che ba/_la la ni sha skam pa/_rtag tu sems can la gnod pa byed pa'o/_/mtshan ma ni 'di lta bu lags te zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_nad pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/shin tu skom pas 'debs par 'gyur/_/
[\[20b.6\]]zhes bya ba ni/_bu chung la sogs pa yin te/_ma yi mngal nas bzung nas ni/_/skyes ma thag tu nad kyis 'debs/_/g.yeng bas btab cing lta bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni g.yeng bas ni byas pas 'jigs pa'o/_/btab cing ni 'dar bas 'jigs pa'o/_/lta bar 'gyur ni 
[\[20b.7\]]mig ldog cing gyen du lta ba'o/_/bzhin yang ni mig dang lus ni dmar ba'o/_/dmar por lta bar 'gyur zhes bya ba ni skyes pa'am/_bud med dam/_khye'u 'am/_kha nas khrag tu skyugs pa'o/_/bskum nas zhes bya ba ni nad pa'i rkang lag 'khums pa'o/_/'khums pa'i 
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]nad kyis ma shi yang glen par 'gro ba'o/_/sa la nyal bar 'gyur zhes bya ba ni/_'gul mi nus pa'o/_/gal te 'gro na rkang lag sa la mi tshugs pa'o/_/kha dog ngan zhing zhes bya ba ni mdze dang phol mig btab pa'o/_/lus kyang skem pa zhes bya ba ni glo'i nad kyis 
[\[21a.2\]]btab pa ste/_dbugs kyang phyi ru 'gro ba'o/_/skra dang sen mo ring ba ste/_/zhes bya ba ni mngal nas skyes ma thag tu na ba ma tshor bar du skra dang sen mo ring ba'o/_/dri ma can la zhes bya ba ni rims kyis btab nas 'khru ba'o/_/dri mi bda' zhes bya ba ni phyi sa bkag pa'o/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]ni 
[\[21a.3\]]don med par slu ba'o/_/kyal par smra ba zhes bya ba ni mdza' bshes rnams la phra ma 'jug pa'o/_/de la zhes bya ba ni grul bum gyi gdon 'dul ba'o/_/gang gis 'dul zhe na/_stong chen mo'i rig sngags kyis gcod pa'o/_/sngags kyi tshig mchis zhes pa ni rgyal po 'phags

[\[21a.5\]]dan zhes bya ba ni 'di lta ste/_yang stong chen mo rab tu 'joms pa'i rig sngags so/_/sngags ni kha kha kha me zhes bya'o/_/rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po/_/zhes bya ba ni grul bum gyi gdon gyi bdag po'o/_ming dang zhes bya ba ni sangs rgyas dang stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma 
[\[21a.6\]]dang /_/rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po'o/_/stobs dang zhes bya ba ni stong chen mo rab tu 'joms pa'i 'o/_/dbang phyug dang zhes bya ba ni/_sangs rgyas kyi dbang phyug gis so/_/byin gyis brlabs zhes bya ba ni/_sems can no/_/bdag gi gdon gnod pa ni grul bum gyi gdon no/_/gnod

[\[21b.1\]]phyag kyang bzhi pa ste/_/sku mdog dmar po me yi 'od/_/g.yas pa sbrul zhags mda' yang bsnams/_/g.yon pa pad+ma gzhu yang bsnams/_/gdan la bzhugs te chu srin la/_/klu yi gdon la mnga' mdzad pa/_/nub kyi ngos nas byin brlabs mdzad/_/klu yi gdon ni nyi shu brgyad/_/klu rnams

[\[21b.3\]]dug_/sad dang rlung dang me yi dug_/bad kan mkhris pa rlung dang ni/_/ldan pa bzhi sbyor phol mig dang /_/g.yan pa me dbal sha bkra dang /_/shu ba mdze dang 'brum bu dang /_/mig nad sna nad kha nad dang /_/rna ba sna dang gag pa dang /_/rma nad klad pa 'bras rnams dang /_/sbos shing 
[\[21b.4\]]skam pa chu ser dang /_/dmu chu skran dang rgyu nad dang /_/glo nad dbugs dang snying nad dang /_/mtsher pa mkhal ma bu snod la/_/bud med bu yang chags mi ster/_/skyes nas 'od 'phrog mngal nas skam/_/mer mer po yang mi ster ro/_/nur nur po na ca le chags/_/grang zhing drod 
[\[21b.5\]]med lus pa yin/_/klu rnams 'dab [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]sa bdag dang /_/sa srin bsen mo klu btsan dang /_/klu yi pho mo klu rgyal dang /_/sa la rgyu ba mthong mi 'gyur/_/rmi lam mthong zhing 'dar bar byed/_/rmi lam mtsho dang chu rnams 'gyur/_/ba glang byis pa bud med 'gyur/_/phag dang

[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]dpa' la/_bskor ba lan gsum byas nas mgo bos phyag 'tshal ba'o/_/bla gos phrag pa gcig tu gzar nas zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das la'o/_/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs te zhes bya ba ni bcom ldan 'das kyi mdun du ste/_yang na ni dge slong

[\[22a.4\]]gi 'khor ro/_/klu 'dab [{]bzangs kyi [,]bzang gi [}]gdon gyis btab pa'i tshul zhes bya ba ni/_mi yi lus la sbrul gyi mgo/_/la la mi yi lus 'dra ba/_/la la bya yi gzugs su ston/_/mtshan ma ni 'di lta bu lags te zhes bya ba ni nad pa'i mtshan nyid 'di lta ste/_'og ma'i tshig go/_/skyigs bus 
[\[22a.5\]]'debs shing zhes bya ba ni dgra rims nad kyis btab cing rlab col zer ba'o/_/dbugs kyang 'byin zhes bya ba ni glo 'byin zhing dbugs nang du sdud mi shes pa'o/_/de bzhin dbugs kyang grang bar 'gyur/_/zhes pa ni/_rims kyis btab cing lus grang zhing /_/lce lkugs rkang lag rengs

[\[22b.1\]]gcong nad kyis btab nas sen mo gzhan la ston pa'o/_/dog sar mchis shing zhes pa ni sa gzhi lta zhing snying mi dga' ba'o/_/ngu bar bgyid pa ni snyod cing snying du 'dre 'jug cing lceb 'dod pa'o/_/de la zhes bya ba ni klu'i gdon tshar gcod pa'o/_/gang gis tshar gcod ce na/_stong chen mo'i

[\[22b.3\]]sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /_stobs kyis byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_zhu ba phul ba'o/_/s+yAd ya the dan zhes bya ba ni/_'di lta ste/_stong chen mo'i rig sngags so/_/kra ka mi zhes bya ba la sogs pa'o/_/rgyal po chen po mig mi bzang zhes bya ba ni klu'i gdon gyi rgyal po'o/_/ming dang

[\[23a.1\]]nyid kyis bdag med pa ni yongs su grub pa ste/_thog ma nyid nas brtags pa des stong pa'i phyir ro/_/de ltar bdag med pa gsum po de nyid kyis kun skrag par byed pa bdag nyid dam/_nyam mi nga ba'i phyir seng ge'i sgra'o/_/bsgrags pa ni 'khor la bshad pa'o/_/da ni

[\[23a.3\]]zhes bya ba la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/dbang po mchog dang mchog ma yin pa'i mkhyen pa'i stobs 'di ni dbang po rnon po de'i rigs can yin/_'di ni rtul po nyan thos kyi rigs can yin pa la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/khams sna tshogs mkhyen pa ni sa bon gyi don te/_'di 
[\[23a.4\]]ni nyan thos kyi rigs sam skal pa'am bag chags can no/_/'di ni rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa chen po la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/mos pa sna tshogs pa mkhyen pa ni gang zag gi nyan thos la mos pa/_'di ni theg pa chen po la mos pa la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/bsam gtan 
[\[23a.5\]]dang /_ting nge 'dzin dang /_rnam par thar pa dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi dang /_kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /_rnam par byang ba mkhyen pa'i stobs ni bsam gtan bzhi dang /_rnam par thar pa brgyad dang /_dpa' bar 'gro ba la sogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin dang /_mthar gyis 
[\[23a.6\]]gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dgu dang /_'khor ba'i chos dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i chos la ma lus pa'i lam mkhyen pa ste/[ {]go rims [,]go rim [}]ltar sbyar ro/_/las kyi rnam par smin pa mkhyen pa ni bsod nams dang bsod nams ma yin pa'i las dang mi g.yo ba'i las kyi 'bras bu rnam 
[\[23a.7\]]par smin pa 'di lta bu dang 'di lta bu thob pa la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/thams cad du 'gro ba'i lam mkhyen pa ni 'dis ni mngon par mtho ba thob pas thams cad kyi lam mo/_/'di ni nyan thos la sogs pa'i nges par legs pa thob cing bgrod par byed pa'i lam la sogs 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]pa mkhyen pa'o/_/sngon gyi gnas rjes su dran pa ni sngon gyi yul 'dir las 'di lta bur blangs te pha ni 'di la ma ni 'di/_'di lta bu la sogs pa zhib tu mkhyen pa'o/_/'chi 'pho ba dang skye ba mkhyen pa ni gang zag 'di dus 'di mi tshe rgyu 'di lta bu zhig gis shi 'phos nas 
[\[23b.2\]]yul 'di'i rigs 'di lta bu zhig tu skyes so zhes bya ba la sogs pa mkhyen pa zag pa zad pa mkhyen pa ni bdag gis sdug bsngal shes so/_/kun 'byung ba spangs so/_/'gog pa mngon du byas so/_/lam rgyud la skyes so zhes bya ba la sogs pa mkhyen pa'o/_/mi 'jigs pa 
[\[23b.3\]]bzhis 'jigs pa med pa zhes bya ba ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi gang zhe na/_bdag gis yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas so zhes dam bcas nas de la chos 'di rnams mngon par sangs ma rgyas so zhes bya bar bdag la dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha 'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs 
[\[23b.4\]]pa'am/_skye dgu'i tshogs gzhan su yang rung ste/_'jig rten na chos dang ldan par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i rgyu yang dag par rjes su ma mthong ste/_rgyu yang dag par rjes su ma mthong bas bdag bde ba thob pa dang /_bag tsha ba med pa thob pa dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob par rnam 
[\[23b.5\]]par spyod pa dang /_khyu mchog rlabs po che'i gnas su dam bcas pa'o/_/'khor gyi nang du seng ge'i sgra sgrog go/_/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo 'jig rten na dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_gzhan su yang rung ste/_chos dang ldan pa bskor bar 
[\[23b.6\]]mi nus pa bdag gis rab tu bskor bar bya'o/_/bdag zag pa zad do zhes dam bcas nas/_de la zag pa 'di rnams yongs su zad par ma gyur to zhes bya bar bdag la dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_skye dgu'i tshogs gzhan 
[\[23b.7\]]su yang rung ste/_chos dang ldan pa bskor bar mi nus pa bdag gis rab tu bskor bar bya'o/_/bdag zag pa zad do zhes dam bcas nas/_de la zag pa 'di rnams yongs su zad par gyur to zhes bya bar bdag la dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_skye dgu'i tshogs gzhan 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]su yang rung ste/_'jig rten na chos dang ldan par 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i rgyu yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ste rgyu yang dag par rjes su ma mthong bas/_de la bdag bde ba thob pa dang bag tsha ba med pa thob pa dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob pa rnam par spyod de/_khyu mchog rlabs 
[\[24a.2\]]po che'i gnas su dam bcas pa'o/_/'khor gyi nang na seng ge'i sgra bsgrag go/_/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo 'jig rten na dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_gzhan sus kyang rung ste/_chos dang ldan pa bskor bar mi nus pa bdag gis rab tu bskor bar 
[\[24a.3\]]bya'o/_/gang yang bdag gis bar chad kyi chos yongs su bstan pa de dag la brten nas/_bar chad kyi chos su 'gyur ro zhes dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_skye dgu'i tshogs gzhan su yang rung ste/_'jig rten na chos dang ldan pa rgol 
[\[24a.4\]]bar 'gyur ro zhes bya ba'i rgyu yang dag par rjes su mi mthong ste/_rgyu yang dag par rjes su ma mthong bas/_de la bdag bde ba thob pas/_bag tsha ba med pa thob pa dang /_mi 'jigs pa thob par rnam par spyod de/_khyu mchog rlabs po che'i gnas su dam bca'o/_/'khor gyi nang du 
[\[24a.5\]]seng ge'i sgra bsgrag go/_/tshangs pa'i 'khor lo 'jig rten na dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_gzhan sus kyang rung ste/_chos dang ldan pa bskor bar mi nus pa bdag gis rab tu bskor bar bya'o/_/gang yang bdag gis lam 'phags pa byung 
[\[24a.6\]]ba rtogs par gyur pa de byed pa'i sdug bsngal yang dag par gyur pa de dag la nan tan byas nas sdug bsngal yang dag par zad par 'gyur ba mi 'byung ngo zhes dge sbyong ngam/_bram ze'am/_lha'am/_bdud dam/_tshangs pa'am/_gzhan su yang rung ste/_'jig rten na chos dang ldan par

[\[25a.1\]]bu yin la zhi ba de thar pa'i srog gcod pa 'phags pa'i nor 'phrogs pas kyang bdud yin no/_/de lta na bdud kyi rigs bye ba phrag dung phyur bco lnga yod pas bar du gcod do/_/des chod na 'khor 'jig rten skyong ba bzhis bar du gcod do/_/de yang gcig sbyin

[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]lo/_/gnod sbyin chad pa ni zhes bya ba ni nad chen po sna tshogs so/_/sdig pa ni sems can thams cad la'o/_/de yi rtsib logs g.yas par ni/_/'bras chen nad kyang 'byung bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni bka' las 'das pa de la'o/_/yang na ni gang gis gnod par byed pa de la'o/_/gnod 
[\[28b.2\]]sbyin nad kyis btab pa na/_/sha bkra'i nad kyis btab par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni dkon mchog gsum la ma dad pa'i gang zag de la gnod pa byas pas/_de'i las kyi rnam par smin pas gnod sbyin ku be ras chad pa chen po bzhi bcad pa'i phyir ro/_/lcang lo can gyi pho brang du/_/gzhar yang 
[\[28b.3\]]'gro bar mi 'gyur ro/_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags 'di'i bka' las 'gal gyur na/_rnam thos kyi bu'i pho brang du 'ong mi ster te/_gnod sbyin rnams kyis chad pas gcod par 'gyur ro/_/lus ngan grogs dang ldan pa yi/_/khyim yang mthong bar mi 'gyur te/_/zhes pa ni 'gro ba 
[\[28b.4\]]lta ci smos te/_mthong bar yang mi ster zhing /_gnod sbyin rnams kyis kyang ring po nas bkag pa'o/_/'byung po mang po tshogs pa na/_/stan yang thob par mi 'gyur ro/_/zhes pa ni gal te pho brang du 'ongs par 'gyur na/_bsdad sa med par skyes te/_'byung po rnams kyis 
[\[28b.5\]]brnyas par byed de/_sdug bsngal mang po myong ba'o/_/gnod sbyin dag gi dkyil 'khor du/_/zas skom dang yang bral bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags kyi bka' las 'das pa'i 'byung po rnams ni 'ongs par gyur kyang mi ster te/_gal te sbyin yang lcags kyi rdog po dang /_/
[\[28b.6\]]me'i phung por mthong ba'o/_/de bas na lha dang /_mi dang /[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dang /_'byung po la sogs pa yang /_rig sngags 'di'i bka' las mi 'da' ba'o/_/stong chen rab 'joms mdo sde la/_/zhes bya ba ni sgrub pa po rten gyi gang zag la la zhig dkon mchog gsum la 
[\[28b.7\]]skyabs su song ba/_stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma/_rdo rje'i rig sngags 'di 'chang ba dang /_klog pa dang /_yid la byed pa dang /_kha ton byed pa dang /_bsgom pa dang /_mchod pa dang /_nyan pa dang /_bshad pa de la/_gnod sbyin 'byung po'i bar chad byed pa de la/_spyi bo 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]dag ni zhes bya ba nas/_de nyid du ni 'khor bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba'i bar na chad pa chen po bdun gcod pa'o/_/sus gcod ce na rdo rje 'chang gis gcod pa'o/_/gnod sbyin 'byung pos bar chad byed pa de la zhes bya ba ni rig sngags 'di'i bka' las 'da' ba 
[\[29a.2\]]dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba rnams la bar chad byas na sdug bsngal thams cad de'i lus la 'byung ngo /_/de la rdo rje 'chang khros te/_/spyi bo dag ni 'gems par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin gyi ded dpon lag na rdo rje khros te/_de'i stobs kyis 'byung po rnams la chad pa 
[\[29a.3\]]bcad pa'o/_/ci'i phyir chad pa gcod ce na/_de'i rnam par smin pas a rdza ka'i dog pa bzhin du tshal pa brgyar 'gas par 'gyur ro/_/spu gri'i so ni rtsub mo yis/_/de yi lce ni gcod par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni dkon mchog gsum gyi bka' las 'das pa dang /_lag na rdo rje'i ye 
[\[29a.4\]]shes kyi spu gris de'i lce gcod pa'o/_/mtshon cha shin tu rnon po yis/_/de yi sna dang rna ba gcod/_/zhes bya ba ni gong ma bzhin du/_de'i rnam smin gyis sna dang lce gcod pa'o/_/'khor lo spu gri'i so 'dra bas/_/klad pa rab tu gshegs par byed/_/ces bya ba ni nam mkha' nas las 
[\[29a.5\]]kyi 'khor lo 'byung bas/_spyi bo nas lte ba'i bar du gcod pa'o/_/tho ba lcags kyis dkris pa yis/_/de yi snying la 'joms par byed/_/ces bya ba ni bar snang nas lcags kyi 'khrul 'khor gyis/_bka' las 'das pa de'i snying ni 'joms pa'o/_/rnag dang khrag ni dron mo dag_/
[\[29a.6\]]de yi kha nas 'dzag par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni/_don snying lnga la nad zhugs shing /_rnag dang khrag rtag tu 'dzag pa'o/_/rig sngags zhags pa'i chad pa yis/_/rtag tu 'khor bar 'gro ba na/_/zhes bya ba ni yang dang yang du rig sngags kyis dmod pa'i zhags pa'i las des rtag tu 
[\[29a.7\]]'khor bar 'khyam pa'o/_/'khor ba gnod sbyin dkyil 'khor du/_/de nyid du ni 'khor bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni de bas na 'di'i bka' las 'da' bar mi bya zhing /_/sems can rnams la gnod pa mi bya'o/_/phyogs bzhi dag tu song gyur nas/_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags 'di la gus 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]pa'i gang zag skyong ba ste/_rgyal po bzhi po dag gis ni phyogs phyogs nas srung ba'o/_/chos kyi go cha bgos gyur te/_/bzang po'i khri la 'dug pa ni/_/zhes pa ni byams pa dang /_snying rje dang /_dga' ba dang /_btang snyoms kyi go cha gyon pa/_seng ge'i gdan la bzhugs nas 
[\[29b.2\]]sems can rnams kyi don byed pa'o/_/shar phyogs na ni yul 'khor srung /_/zhes bya ba ni dri za'i ded dpon nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang ldan pas/_shar phyogs skyong ba'o/_/lho phyogs na ni 'phags skyes po/_/zhes bya ba ni grul bum gyi ded dpon nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang ldan pas lho phyogs 
[\[29b.3\]]skyong ba'o/_/nub phyogs na ni mig mi bzang /_/zhes bya ba ni klu'i ded dpon nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang ldan pas/_nub phyogs skyong ba'o/_/byang phyogs na ni lus ngan po/_/zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin gyi ded dpon nyi shu rtsa brgyad dang bcas pas/_byang phyogs skyong ba'o/_/dpal dang 
[\[29b.4\]]gzi brjid 'bar ba yi/_/rgyal po dag ni phyin pa dang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'jig rten du dpal dang ldan zhing /_sde bo che dpung bzhi'i bdag po ste/_de'i gzi brjid kyis gling bzhi khyab pa'o/_/phyin pa ni khyab pa'o/_/de'i tshe ston pa thams cad mkhyen/_/bar snang gnas la rab tu 
[\[29b.5\]]'phags/_/zhes bya ba ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyang /_sems can gyi don la gnas pa ste/_nor bu rin po che dang 'dra bar 'jig rten gyi khams rang gi lag mthil du shes pa/_sems can rnams las 'phags pa'o/_/tshangs pas sprul pa'i gzhal 
[\[29b.6\]]med khang /_/der ni rdo rje'i gdan la bzhugs/_/zhes bya ba ni tshad med pa bzhi bsgoms pas tshangs pa ste/_gzhal med khang ni sku gsung thugs kyi rang bzhin lha'i sku'o/_/yang na gzhal med khang ni ri rab chen por snang ba 'jig rten gyi khams tsam mo/_/de lta bu'i gzhal yas 
[\[29b.7\]]khang gi nang na yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin snang ba'o/_/de nas tshangs dang tshangs chen rnams/_/thal mo sbyar 'khod phyag 'tshal lo/_/zhes bya ba ni de lta bu'i yon tan dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas shAkya thub pa la/_mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa dang /_
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]tshangs chen rnams kyis gus par phyag 'tshal ba'o/_/dpal ldan gser gyi ri bo 'dra/_/gser gyi mchod sdong dang yang mtshungs/_/zhes bya ba ni phyi nang med par gser gyi yon tan dang ldan te/_mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan zhing dpe byad bzang po brgyad 
[\[30a.2\]]cus brgyan pa/_'dzam bu'i gling gi gser gyi kha dog dang 'dra zhing /_yon tan gyis brgyan pa'o/_/me tog pad+ma bzhin du rgyas/_/sA la'i rgyal po bzhin du rgyas/_/zhes bya ba ni zhal pad+ma bzhin du sems can rnams la bstan pa/_shing sA la bzhin du zil gyis non pa'o/_/nyi ma 
[\[30a.3\]]dang 'dra zla nya la/_/skar ma rnams kyis yongs bskor zhing /_/zhes bya ba ni dregs pa ni nyi ma lta bu ste/_'jam pa ni zla ba nya ba'i 'od zer lta bu'o/_/byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa 'khor rnam bzhi ni skar ma lta bu'o/_/thub sku gser gyi kha dog la/_/mtshan rnams 
[\[30a.4\]]kyis ni yongs su bskor/_/zhes bya ba ni sku gsung thub cing 'dzam bu'i gling gi gser gyi kha dog 'dra ba/_sku la mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa/_dpe byad bzang po brgyad cus brgyan pa'o/_/'jig rten mes po tshangs pa yis/_/'jig rten sgron ma bstod byas te/_/zhes 
[\[30a.5\]]bya ba ni stong spyi phud kyi rgyal po tshangs pas kyang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyis/_sems can thams cad 'khor ba nas sgrol ba/_stong gsum gyi chos kyi rgyal po la phyag 'tshal zhing bstod do/_/'jig rten mgon po'i spyan sngar ni/_/'jig rten skyong la 'di skad smras/_/
[\[30a.6\]]zhes bya ba ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi gtso bo ste/_sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa bsrung ba/_phyogs skyong bcus kyang zhus pa'o/_/'jig rten skyong gi 'khor rnams kyis/_/rjes su bstan pa ma thob ste/_/zhes bya ba ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos 
[\[30a.7\]]'di/_yid bzhin nor bu rin po che 'dra ba ste/_'jig rten skyong ba dag gis kyang 'di dang gzhan du mtshungs pa med pa'o/_/gang phyir 'di las sangs rgyas dang /_/rang sangs rgyas dag 'byung 'gyur zhing /_/zhes bya ba ni stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma'i lam 'di nas/_rin po che shel dang 
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]'dra ba ste/_sangs rgyas la sogs pa gang yang 'byung ba'o/_/'di las nyan thos rnams kyang skye/_/lha rnams dag kyang skye bar 'gyur/_/zhes pa ni nam mkha' las char byung ba bzhin du/_'di'i sgo nas gang yang 'byung ba'o/_/rig byed yan lag drug mthar phyin/_/bram ze rnams kyang 
[\[30b.2\]]skye bar 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni rtag pa'i chos drug yin te/_de yang sngar bshad pa bzhin du lta ba drug go/_/de rnams mthar phyin cing grol ba ste/_'di'i sgo nas skye gnas bzang po bram ze'i rigs thob pa'o/_/drang srong skal ba chen po dang /_/dge sbyong bram ze 'di las skye/_/zhes bya ba 
[\[30b.3\]]ni stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma'i sgo nas lta ba thams cad 'byung ba'o/_/khyed cag snying las chung byed pa/_/mi yi skye dgu gnod par 'gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni ma rungs pa'i 'byung po rnams 'jigs skrag byed pa/_mi rnams la byams pa dang /_snying rjer ldan pa'o/_/tshangs pa yi ni 
[\[30b.4\]]tshig thos nas/_/'jig rten skyong gis tshig smras pa/_/zhes bya ba ni mi mjed kyi bdag po tshangs pa'i tshig thos nas/_'byung po rnams la phyogs skyong ba bcus smras pa'o/_/tshangs pa dang ni thub chen po/_/zhes bya ba ni sangs rgyas dang /_byang chub sems dpa' dang /_nyan thos dang /_rang 
[\[30b.5\]]sangs rgyas dang /_sems can thams cad bskyed pa'i ma stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma'i rig sngags 'di yin pa'i phyir ro/_/bdag gis ri rab bsgul byas shing /_/zhes bya ba ni phung po'i gnas skabs so/_/ri rab de yi rgya dang ni/_/'phang dang grwa bzhi'i zur rnams dang /_/zheng 
[\[30b.6\]]la khru bzhi 'phang la bzhi/_/mu khyud zur brgyad sho 'dra ba/_/steng du dkyil 'khor legs par bri/_/sa tshon lngas ni yang dag tu/_/stong chen rab 'joms dkyil 'khor yang /_/gru bzhi sgo bzhi ka ba brgyad/_/twa ra rnam bzhi rta babs rgyan/_/dra phyed rin chen la sogs pa/_/
[\[30b.7\]]gdugs dang rgyal mtshan ba dan rnams/_/'dod pa'i yon tan lha mo dang /_/mkhas pa dag gis bri bar bya/_/'bras sam bag phye la sogs pas/_/mkhas pa dag gis yang dag bya/_/ri rab gzugs ni sho 'dra ba/_/lha dang lha min 'byung po'i gnas/_/de lta bu yi gnas 
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]bcas la/_/lha dang lha min 'byung po dang /_/de rnams la ni dbul bar bya/_/rgyal po dang ni rgyal po'i 'khor/_/blon po dang ni blon mo dang /_/gang dag de rnams gnod pa dang /_/'byung po [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]sogs gnod dang /_/byad stems dang ni 'thor ba dang /_/
[\[31a.2\]]ro langs sbod gtong gtong ba dang /_/zos pa dang ni skyugs pa dang /_/grib ma ngan dang ltas ngan dang /_/bri nyes dang ni bsgom nyes dang /_/smad nad dang ni nyin gcig dang /_/nyin gnyis gsum pa bzhi pa dang /_/nyin bdun zla phyed zla ba gcig_/nyin mo ba dang yud tsam 
[\[31a.3\]]dang /_/ldang dub pa dang 'byung po dang /_/mi yi rims dang mi ma yin/_/rlung dang mkhris pa bad kan dang /_/'dus pa bzhi 'gyur dag dang ni/_/de yi cho ga yang dag yin/_/nad ni bzhi brgya rtsa bzhir gyur pa las/_/rmi lam ngan dang mtshan ma ngan/_/sngags btang ba dang bzlog pa 
[\[31a.4\]]dang /_/ma mo zor kha phur kha dang /_/gshin rje la sogs gang yang rung /_/de bzlog cho ga yang dag yin/_/shu ba g.yan pa mdze dang ni/_/phol mig gzhang nad 'bras dang ni/_/lhog dang me dbal bzlog pa dang /_/sha bkra dmu chu khong nad dang /_/[{]glad pa [,]klad pa [}]na ba la sogs dang /_/de yi cho ga 
[\[31a.5\]]yang dag yin/_/gtso bor gyur pa nyi shu brgyad/_/stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa brgyad phrag dang ni/_/'byung po rnams kyi cho ga yang dag bshad/_/ri rab la sogs bzhi dag la'ang /_/dkar po dang ni mthing ga dang /_/dmar po dang ni ser po rnams/_/shar phyogs ngos nas rim bzhin du/_/
[\[31a.6\]]yang dag gis ni shes par bya/_/shar phyogs lha dang drang srong dang /_/dri za'i gdon rnams gza' dang bcas/_/stong phrag brgyad cu nyi shu rtsa brgyad phrag_/de rnams shar phyogs gdon rnams so/_/grul bum la sogs ma mo dang /_/srin mo [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]gdon rnams dang /_/lho phyogs la 
[\[31a.7\]]ni yang dag shes/_/stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa brgyad phrag_/de dag lho phyogs gdon rnams so/_/klu dang 'dab [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]sa bdag dang /_/nub phyogs dag ni yang dag shes/_/stong phrag brgyad cu nyi shu rtsa brgyad phrag_/de dag nub phyogs gdon rnams so/_/gnod sbyin pho mo la sogs dang /_/
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]klu btsan la sogs gzhan rnams dang /_/byang phyogs dag ni yang dag shes/_/stong phrag brgyad cu nyi shu rtsa brgyad phrag_/de dag byang phyogs gdon rnams so/_/shar lho lho nub nub byang dang /_/byang shar steng dang 'og dang ni/_/me yi gdon dang bden bral dang /_/rlung lha'i gdon dang 'byung 
[\[31b.2\]]po'i gdon/_/tshangs pa la sogs sa bdag dang /_/stong phrag brgyad cu nyi shu rtsa brgyad phrag_/mtshan drug la ni yang dag shes/_/steng dang 'og dang zur rnams su/_/'byung po'i tshogs ni shes par bya/_/bag phye dang ni 'bras phye'am/_/gzugs ni sna tshogs mkhas par bya/_/ba dan 
[\[31b.3\]]dang ni rgyal mtshan dang /_/gdugs dang me tog phreng ba dang /_/'byung po'i tshogs dang bcas pa dang /_/bu ram zho dang sbrang rtsi dang /_/me tog dkar po la sogs dang /_/sha dang khrag dang chang dang ni/_/nya sha sgog pa la sogs dang /_/me tog ser po'i phreng ba rnams/_/'o ma ka ra 
[\[31b.4\]]mngar gsum dang /_/mar la sogs pa'i sman rnams dang /_/me tog dmar po dUr ba'i phreng ba dang /_/chang dang sha dang khrag rnams dang /_/la phug la sogs me tog ljang gu'i phreng ba dang /_/dmar po nag po mthing ga dang /_/sna tshogs me tog phreng ba dang /_/me dang bden bral 
[\[31b.5\]]rlung dang ni/_/dbang ldan tshangs pa sa bdag dang /_/sngon gyi rdzas la cha drug tu/_/yang dag gis ni shes rab la/_/mkhas pa'i sems la shes par bya/_/a kar gu gul la sogs dang /_/sbrul gyi pags pa nya mgo dang /_/yungs kar sgog pa bsres pa dang /_/ga bur gla rtsi 
[\[31b.6\]]spang spos dang /_/tsan+dan la sogs sbyar ba dang /_/tshil bu yungs kar bsres pa dang /_/de dag la ni spos phor ro/_/khur ba chan dang thug pa dang /_/de dag kun la shes par bya/_/rgya phibs bla re gdugs dang bcas/_/rgyal mtshan ba dan sna tshogs dang /_/spos sbyar yang dag me 
[\[31b.7\]]tog dang /_/bu ram zhal zas sna tshogs dang /_/lhan cig sbyar ba'i dri rnams dang /_/de rnams la ni yang dag mchod/_/sna tshogs pad+ma'i dbus su ni/_/stong chen rab tu 'joms ma yang /_/pad+ma'i lte bar yang dag bzhugs/_/de yi phyi rol ra ba la/_/'khor lo rtsibs brgyad shes 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]par bya/_/rgyal po bzhi dang phyogs skyong dang /_/gza' dang skar ma la sogs la/_/'khor lo lha rnams sgo rnams la/_/mkhas pas yang dag shes par bya/_/lnga'am gcig gam gang 'byor yang /_/gang ba'i bum pa bkra shis pa/_/lnga pa'i dbus su 
[\[32a.2\]]gzhag par bya/_/gser ram dngul las byas pa yi/_/'jim sogs rab gnas byas pa yi/_/mchod rten la ni mchod par bya/_/mtshams gcod yul 'khor srung ba dang /_/bkra shis la sogs gang yang rung /_/de bzhin du ni byas nas ni/_/the tshom med de yang dag yin/_/slob 
[\[32a.3\]]dpon lnga'am gcig kyang rung /_/yang dag gis ni rim pa bzhin/_/'byung po rnams ni dgug par bya/_/stong chen rab tu 'joms ma yang /_/phyogs bzhi'i mtshams su rgyun mi 'chad/_/slob dpon dgongs pa gsum ldan pas/_/ngag ma 'dres par bzlas par bya/_/zhes bstan pa'o/_/
[\[32a.4\]]sa yang rnam par bzlog byas la/_/zhes bya ba ni phyogs skyong ba bcus tshangs pa dang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi spyan sngar zhus pa'o/_/stobs dang ldan zhing ri rab thogs par nus te/_sa gzhi yang 'khrugs par nus shing /_/'jig rten gyi khams kyang ldog par 
[\[32a.5\]]nus pa'o/_/rgya mtsho la ni thug pa rnams/_/rnam par sbyang ba nyid du bya/_/zhes bya ba ni shar phyogs nas [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin du/_phyi'i kho ra khor yug tsam bdag gi dbang du 'gyur te/_steng dang 'og tu yang de bzhin no/_/'jig rten khams de sbyong bar byed cing 'dul bas na sbyang ba'o/_/
[\[32a.6\]]zhags pa dam po ma nyams pas/_/nyi ma zla ba rlung rnams dang /_/zhes bya ba ni mdo sde zhags pas de dang de ldan pas yang 'dren pa'o/_/skar ma dag ni thams cad kyang /_/bdag gis su ni bcing bar bya/_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags kyi dmod mo'i zhags pas/_/smin drug la sogs 
[\[32a.7\]]pa'i rgyu skar rnams bcing ba'o/_/gang dag rab tu sdang byed cing /_/'jig rten la ni phan mi byed/_/ces bya ba ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams su rgyu zhing /_/gnod pa skyel ba rnams la rig sngags kyi bcing ba lngas bcings pa'o/_/de dag la ni thams cad du/_/
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]phyogs kyang snang bar mi bya'o/_/zhes bya ba ni/_gnod pa skyel ba'i 'byung po rnams/_phyogs bcu rnams su gnas pa'i sa med par/_/sdug bsngal drag po myong ba'o/_/'jig rten lha dang bcas pa de/_/'byung po rnams kyi phyir smod de/_/zhes bya ba ni lha dang klu dang lha ma yin la 
[\[32b.2\]]sogs pa/_bgegs rigs stong phrag brgyad cu po rnams/_phyir 'gyed cing 'dor mi gtong ba'o/_/'byung po mi yi skye dgu la/_/gnod par byed cing 'tshe bar byed/_/ces bya ba ni phung po lnga'i rang bzhin/_byang chub sems la gnod pa'o/_/gang dag sngags dang sman rnams dang /_/
[\[32b.3\]]rig sngags las ni 'da' byed pa/_/zhes bya ba ni 'di la gus par byed cing dkon mchog gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba'i gang zag dang /_sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed ces rig sngags kyi bka' las 'das pa rnams so/_/'di la sngags kyi dam pa ni/_/pha rol gnon pa ston par 
[\[32b.4\]]byed/_/ces bya ba ni rig sngags kyi rgyal mo stong chen mo rab tu 'joms ma 'dis kyang /_gsang sngags kyi tshig ston pa tsam gyis ni/_ma rungs pa'i 'byung po rnams kyi stobs 'phrog pa'o/_/rig sngags las ni 'da' ba'i 'khor/_/kun du chad pas bsdigs byas te/_/zhes pa ni phyag 
[\[32b.5\]]mtshan dang sku mdog ston pa ni/_ded dpon dag ni nyi shu brgyad/_/re re la yang stong phrag brgyad cu brgyad cu'o/_/'di dag gi sku mdog dang phyag mtshan ni/_dkar po dmar po ser po dang /_/ljang gu dang ni mthing ga dang /_/la la dkar ser dmar ser dang /_/la la ser nag ljang nag dang /_/
[\[32b.6\]]mgo bo ser po mjug ma nag_/smad dkar stod gnag khra bo'i gzugs/_/sna tshogs gzugs te phyed gzugs so/_/lag na rdo rje 'khor lo thogs/_/gtun shing dang ni rtse gsum mo/_/la la ral gri lag na thogs/_/gtor ma rwa dang mdung thung thogs/_/'phang mdung dang ni nar 
[\[32b.7\]]tsa dang /_/la la dbyug pa thogs pa dang /_/rtse gcig dang ni mdung [{]rings [,]ring [}]thogs/_/mdung dang mda' gzhu lcags kyu dang /_/la la mda' [{]rings [,]ring [}]ga ge dang /_/cha lang dang ni rol mo'i tshogs/_/pi wang gling bu glu len cing /_/gnod sbyin pho mo glu len cing /_/yan lag lnga dang ldan pa yis/_/
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]rol mo'i tshogs ni la la byed/_/la la mi lpags gon pa dang /_/mi mgo lag na thogs pa dang /_/mi yi rgyu mas thod kyang byed/_/la la mi yi rus pa thogs/_/sna tshogs rgyan dang sna tshogs phyag mtshan dang /_/'byung po'i tshogs su shes par bya/_/sku mdog dang phyag mtshan 
[\[33a.2\]]bstan pa'o/_/'jig rten mgon po'i spyan snga ru/_/thal sbyar 'khod nas 'di skad smras/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po'i gnas ston pa'o/_/skya snar bu ni ka ba na/_/grong khyer bzang na byang phyogs na/_/bya rgod phung po'i rgyal po'i khab/_/rgya mtsho mtha' yi bar gyi sar/_/lan gsum bar 
[\[33a.3\]]du 'gro byed grong /_/mkha' lding rgyas pa sbed pa nas/_/rgyal khab dang ni ser skya'i gnas/_/shAkya'i tog na gar ltam bar/_/sgra gcan dang ni tsir da na/_/mnyan yod dang ni gnas dang bcas/_/rgya mtsho'i gnas dang yangs pa can/_/gyad yul dang ni g.yog 'dor dang /_/tsam pa

[\[33a.7\]]brlag na dang lang ka dang /_/rab nyams dang ni mdzod 'dzin dang /_/rnam rgyal ldan dang bcom brlag na yang gnas/_/ma la ya dang rtse 'dzin dang /_/po 'dra na dang rab tu gnas/_/pi tang ka le dang ni rlabs ldan dang /_/sna nas thung dang gso ba'i mtha'/_/dga' byed dang ni lag pa dang /_/

[\[33b.1\]]pu mtshan dang ni mdzod 'dzin dang /_/pho brang ngan dang g.yog 'khor dang /_/'gram ldan phung po grong khyer drug_/mtha' ma na dang srung ba can/_/ba lang 'jog dang phyogs mtshams na/_/gdugs 'dra na dang grong khyer gsum/_/tshang tshing gcig dang u dum+WAr/_/kau shAm+bI dang zhi ldan na/_/

[\[33b.4\]]'gro ldan dag dang ser skya'i gnas/_/gzhan snyas dang ni sgo gnas dang /_/dbu ma'i sde dang bzang mdzes dang /_/grong khyer snying dang mya ngan sa/_/khyus 'gro dang ni nga rgyal brag_/'jig byed dang ni kha che'i yul/_/ral pa can dang grong dang ni/_/kha che'i yul gyi sa mtshams na/_/sa la

[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]na/_pho brang lcang lo can du skye ba thob pa'o/_/'di bshad pa na mi nyan pa/_/de dag chad pa bshad par bya/_/zhes bya ba ni yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi bka'/_/rig sngags kyi bden pa'i dmod mos rlag par byed pa'o/_/tshangs pas sprul pa'i mdo sde ste/_/sangs rgyas

[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]ba ni dbang phyug chen po'i 'khor rnams zhes bya ba ni ded dpon du gnas shing sde che ba'o/_/bcing ba rnam pa lngas bsdams nas/_/mdo sde'i zhags pas kun du drangs/_/zhes bya ba ni 'dod pa'i yon tan lngas bsdams shing /_rig sngags kyi dmod mo'i zhags pas 
[\[36a.2\]]bsdams pa'o/_/'byung po 'joms pa'i ri la ni/_/'byung po thams cad lhags nas su/_/zhes bya ba ni rigs mi gcig pa'i gdon thams cad gnas pa'o/_/'joms pa ni rig sngags kyis 'joms pa'o/_/rig sngags thams cad 'byung ba'i gnas/_/rig sngags 'di ni kun du 
[\[36a.3\]]bsam/_/zhes bya ba ni nam mkha' bzhin thams cad la khyab pa/_sems can dang byang chub sems dpa' la sogs pa skyed pa'i yum lta bu'o/_/chad pa mi bzad drag po ste/_/gang phyir sangs rgyas rnams kyis gsungs/_/zhes bya ba ni 'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang da ltar gyi sangs

[\[36a.7\]]chu lu ma dang /_/dba' klong dag na gang 'khod dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de dang ldan pa'i gnas su bsdad cing /_sems can rnams la gnod pa byed pa'o/_/skyed mos tshal dang gzhal med khang /_/kun dga' dang ni nags rnams dang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po rnams gnas gcig tu mi 
[\[36b\]]
[\[36b.1\]]gnas te/_gzugs sna tshogs su snang ba'o/_/mchod rten gnas dang grong gi gnas/_/shing ljon drung na gang gnas dang /_/zhes bya ba ni gnas de dang de la gnas shing /_'jig rten khams su rgyu ba'o/_/grong khyer sgo dang grong rnams dang /_/grong rdal dang ni ljongs rnams dang /_/

[\[36b.3\]]glags tshol ba'o/_/dkyil 'khor dag dang dur khrod dang /_/de bzhin lha khang rnams dang ni/_/zhes bya ba ni tshan gcig ni lha khang rnying pa la chags shing dge ba la bar chod byed pa'o/_/mtshams dang sho gam gnas rnams dang /_/khang stong dang ni gram sa dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de dang der 
[\[36b.4\]]ldan pa'i gnas su bsdad cing /_gzugs mi gcig par snang ba'o/_/gnod sbyin gang rnams bla 'og dang /_/phyogs bzhi dang ni mtshams rnams nas/_/zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin de dang ldan pa ni/_/phyogs mtshams brgyad du gnas pa'o/_/gnod sbyin bye ba stong rnams ni/_/
[\[36b.5\]]rig sngags zhags pas bkug pa rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni/_stong phrag de dang de yod pa/_/ma rungs pa yi 'byung po rnams/_/rig sngags kyi dmod mo'i zhags pas 'dren pa'o/_/rdza rnga dag ni brdungs nas su/_/la las rdza zlum dag ni brdungs/_/zhes bya ba ni sgra la dga' ba'i

[\[36b.7\]]rnams byas nas su/_/zhes bya ba ni sems can rnams slu ba'o/_/dbang po zla ba chu lha dang /_/ba ra d+h+wa dza skye dgu'i bdag_/ces pa ni gnod sbyin gyi ded dpon sum cu rtsa gnyis su gnas shing /_/sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa srung ba dag go/_/mi ldan pa dang kun du lta/_/mngon shes 
[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]gangs can rdzogs pa dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de rnams la yang de'i sder bcas shing /_phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgyu ba'o/_/tsan+dan dang ni 'dod mchog dang /_/mgul pa mdzes dang mgul nges dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de rnams kyis kyang sems can gyi don 
[\[37a.2\]]byas shing /_rig sngags la gus pa'o/_/skye dgu'i bla mar gyur pa dang /_/lha las byung ba'i ma ldan dang /_/zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin gyi tshan de dang der ldan zhing /_nam mkha' dang /_sa steng sa 'og tu rgyu ba'o/_/dri za sna tshogs sde dang ni/_/mi rgyal rgyal ba khyu mchog dang /_/zhes bya 
[\[37a.3\]]ba ni sde bo cher grags shing stobs dang ldan pa'o/_/de bzhin phud bu lnga zhes bya/_/g.yer ma dang ni nyi zer dang /_/zhes bya ba ni nyi ma'i 'od dang ldan pa/_yud tsam gyis stong gsum khyab pa'o/_/nya lcibs dang ni ral gyi bu/_/kun gyi bshes dang grags 'dzin dang /_/zhes bya 
[\[37a.4\]]ba ni 'byung po sde tshan de dang ldan pa/_ral gri bzhin du rno ba'o/_/'brog gnas dang ni yid [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang /_/khab rna dang ni ri sul kha/_/zhes bya ba ni sde bo che nyi shu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa/_de rnams yang gzugs sna tshogs dang ldan pa'o/_/lnga 'dzin dang ni khab [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag_/
[\[37a.5\]]sde dang dpung dang bzhon par bcas/_/zhes bya ba ni stobs dang ldan pa sde bo cher grags pa'o/_/lha dang klu dang dri za dang /_/gnod sbyin 'byung po lha ma yin/_/zhes bya ba ni gdon rigs mi gcig pa lha'i gdon la sogs pa stong phrag brgyad cu'o/_/nyin gsum pa yi smyo byed dang /_/de 
[\[37a.6\]]bzhin bzhi pa'i rims dag dang /_/zhes bya ba ni nad sna tshogs gtong zhing smyo bar byed pa rnams yin te/_de rnams yang gzhan gyis mi thub pa stobs dang ldan pa'o/_/'jig rten 'tshe ba gang yin pa/_/gnod sbyin 'byung po ma rungs rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 
[\[37a.7\]]'jig rten gyi khams skad cig yud tsam thang cig gis rgyu ba/_/sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed pa'o/_/phyogs bzhi nas ni bkug nas su/_/bcing ba lnga yis bsdams pa rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni ma rungs pa'i 'byung po rnams la/_ye shes lnga'i zhags pas bcings 
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]pa'o/_/'jig rten mgon po'i spyan snga ru/_/thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad smras/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po de rnams kyis kyang yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi spyan sngar/_gus par byas nas zhus pa'o/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpa' khyod la/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la/_/
[\[37b.2\]]zhes bya ba ni bdud bzhi 'joms pa la dpa' ba/_skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa/_dpe byad bzang po brgyad cu pos sku la brgyan pa/_sems can rnams la mnyam pa nyid du lta ba/_snying rje dang byams pas khyab pa/_thal mo sbyar nas phyag 'tshal lo/_/chos rgyal khyod la 
[\[37b.3\]]phyag 'tshal lo/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po de rnams kyis zhabs la gus par phyag 'tshal te/_stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams la/_chos kyi rgyal srid mdzad pa'o/_/de dag gi ni mdun rgyug pa/_/'byung po gnod sbyin lus po che/_/zhes bya ba ni che ba ri rab dang mnyam pa/_chung 
[\[37b.4\]]ba ba spu'i rtse mo tsam/_de dang der ldan pa'i 'byung po rnams rgyu ba'o/_/lag pa bzhi pa ma rungs pa/_/rkang [{]mangs [,]mang [}]dang ni rkang gcig pa/_/zhes bya ba ni mda' dang gzhu dang ral gri mdung rnams thogs pa/_stong tsho gcig gi rkang pa yod pa/_rkang gcig ni gnyis sbrel nas rgyu ba'o/_/rkang 
[\[37b.5\]]bzhi pa dang rkang gnyis dang /_/rkang pa bsgreng la spyi'u tshugs dang /_/zhes bya ba ni ma rungs pa'i 'byung po yang spyi bo'i gtsug gis 'gro zhing /_sems can rnams 'jigs par byed pa'o/_/de bzhin du rkang bzhi rkang gnyis kyang 'jigs par byed pa'o/_/lus mang mgo bo gcig pa dang /_/lus 
[\[37b.6\]]gcig pa la mgo bzhi dang /_/zhes bya ba ni gzugs che zhing 'jigs su rung ba bzhin mi sdug pa rnams so/_/mig mang po la lus phyed dang /_/mig gcig lto ni bcu gnyis dang /_/zhes bya ba ni rkang gcig lag gcig lus su snang zhing /_lus de la mig mang po yod pa ste/_'byung po 
[\[37b.7\]]gzhan yang lto ba ri bo lta bur gnas pa/_mang po bsdebs pa mig gcig tu snang ba'o/_/bong bu rnga mo glang chen mgo/_/lag pa bsgreng la mgo btud dang /_/zhes bya ba ni gzugs mi'i gzugs la mgo bo sna tshogs su snang ba/_rkang pa dang lag pa sgreng bar byed pa'o/_/mtshon cha'i so dang 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]mtshon cha'i lag_/mtshon cha'i rkang pa srin po rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni de kun gyi yan lag dang ral gri dang /_mda' gzhu dang mdung la sogs par snang ba'o/_/zangs kyi skra dang zangs kyi so/_/zangs kyi mig dang zangs kyi lag_/ces bya ba ni shin tu dmar ba 'jigs su rung 
[\[38a.2\]]ba ste/_mtshon cha bzhin du snang ba'o/_/zangs kyi tho ba rkang pa dang /_/zangs kyi sna dang zangs kyi kha/_/zhes bya ba ni mtshon cha mi 'jigs pa 'dzin pa/_rkang pa yang tho ba'i tshul du gnas pa'o/_/de bzhin du kha la sogs pa yang zangs kyi skra bzhin du snang ba'o/_/rkang lag kun du 'bar ba 
[\[38a.3\]]dang /_/mi yi lus su gnas pa rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni me bzhin du 'bar te/_des kyang mi la zhe sdang la sogs pa 'bar bar byed pa'o/_/sgur zhar lba ba gsum yod pa/_/mig mi sdug la kha glal ba/_/zhes bya ba ni bong thung zhing lus po sgur te/_de'i ske la lba ba gsum yod pa/_
[\[38a.4\]]lus po zha zhing kha gdangs pa/_mchu sngon por snang ba 'jigs pa'o/_/chu srin stag gi gzugs dang ni/_/gzugs mi sdug dang kha ral po/_/zhes bya ba ni mi'i lus la mgo brnyan du yod pa/_kha gdangs shing mi sdug pa'o/_/mchu 'phyang po dang so yon po/_/rab tu sdug pa'i khro gnyer 
[\[38a.5\]]can/_/zhes bya ba ni gdong pa yo ba/_de bzhin du mchu dang so yo ba'o/_/khro bar gnas shing sems can la 'jigs par byed pa'o/_/gsus po che dang bum sna po/_/rna 'phyangs po dang rna ba med/_/ces bya ba ni gsus pa ri rab tsam snang ba/_rna ba gos su gon pa/_rna ba bcad 
[\[38a.6\]]pa'i 'byung po rnams so/_/lag ring dang ni rna ba ring /_/sna yi rtse dang lag rtse ring /_/zhes bya ba ni lag pa brkyang bas dpag tshad brgyar slebs te/_snas lus khebs te glang po che'i sna dang 'dra ba/_de bzhin sor mo yang ring ba'o/_/lus skams lus ni ring ba dang /_/skra ring rab tu 
[\[38a.7\]]brgyan pa dang /_/zhes bya ba ni shin tu nag pa/_shing lpags skams pa dang 'dra ba/_/ring ba ni ring ba pas kyang ches ring ba lus rnams kyang skra ring bas brgyan pa'o/_/rkang pa mid pa phra ba dang /_/dri nga ba dang bum lto po/_/zhes bya ba ni yan lag gi tshigs rdog por yod pa/_rkang 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]pa phra bas te/_dri mi zhim pa mnam pa/_gsus pa ri bo tsam yod pa'o/_/yan lag chu srin 'dra ba dang /_/gtun shing tho ba 'dra ba'i lto/_/zhes bya ba ni lag pa dang rkang pa yang chu srin 'dra ste/_gzugs ring ba/_rked pa nyag pa/_nag por snang ba'o/_/gnam lto ba dang sna bo 
[\[38b.2\]]che/_/gyen du brdzes skra rab tu dmar/_/zhes bya ba ni sna yar 'khyil pa glang po che'i sna 'dra ba/_khab kyi rtse mo dang 'dra ba'i skra dmar zhing 'bar ba'o/_/mgo bo che la gzhu mgrin po/_/rked pa sgur po bum lto bo/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po la la ni gzhu bzhin du ske sgur bas te/_de bzhin du 
[\[38b.3\]]yan lag dang lus po rnams kyang sgur ba'o/_/ri rab rtse mo bsgul nas su/_/me mdag gi ni char pa 'bebs/_/zhes bya ba ni gzi brjid dang /_stobs dang ldan zhing /_ri rab dang sa g.yo bar byed pa ma rungs pa'i char 'bebs pa'o/_/ljon shing ri dang rdo ba dang /_/sprin gyi tshogs kyi 
[\[38b.4\]]mdog 'dra ba/_/zhes bya ba ni la la gzugs bzang bar gnas pa/_gzhan rnams yang bzhin mi sdug pa'o/_/dung dang rnga chen rdza rnga dang /_/grul bum rnga bran rnams kyi sgra/_/zhes bya ba ni sgra dang rol mo la dga' ba gar sna tshogs dang ldan pa'o/_/'jigs pa'i sgra ni rab tu 'byin/_/mid 
[\[38b.5\]]po che la bong bu'i sgra/_/zhes bya ba ni ri rab kyi rtse mo nas gling bzhir thos pa'i sgra/_sgra des sems can 'jigs pa'o/_/nag po sngon po ser po dang /_/dmar ser me ltar dmar ser dang /_/zhes bya ba ni bzhin mi sdug pa gzugs sna tshogs su snang ba/_bskal ba'i me'i 'od dang 'dra ba'i 'od 
[\[38b.6\]]zer snang ba'o/_/khab 'dra'i spu dang ral gri'i skra/_/dmar po'i khrag gis bskus pa rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni shin tu rno ba lus dang mgo rnams la khyab pa/_kha yang khrag gis bskus pa'o/_/mi ro dag ni khyer nas su/_/za bzhin du ni rnam par rgyug_/ces bya ba ni mi rnams la 'tshe bar byed 
[\[38b.7\]]cing srog 'phrog pa rnams yin te/_glags tshol zhing 'phrang kha bsgugs pa rnams yin no/_/so rnon lag pa dmar po dang /_/mchu ni khrag gis bskus pa dang /_/zhes bya ba ni mche ba ring ba mtshon 'dra ba ste/_sems can rnams kyi lag pa dmar por snang ba/_khrag 'thung ba la dga' ba'o/_/
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]lus phyed dag ni zos pa dang /_/mkhal ma snying dang nang grol gyis/_/zhes bya ba ni sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed pas/_sha dang khrag la dga' ba dang /_/don snying lnga la dga' ba'o/_/snyim pa shin tu bkang ba dang /_byis pa dag ni za bzhin du/_/zhes bya ba ni mi rnams kyi 
[\[39a.2\]]ni srog 'phrogs nas don snying rnam pa lnga phyung nas za bar byed pa'o/_/mer mer po dang srog chags kyi/_/rkang lag rnams kyi stobs kyang 'phrog_/ces bya ba ni byang chub sems chags su mi ster bar za ba/_ma'i mngal nas gzi brjid 'phrog pa'o/_/keng rus kyi ni lus kyis su/_/skye bo 
[\[39a.3\]]mang po skrag par byed/_/ces bya ba ni pags pa dang sha med pa dang rus par snang ste/_de lta bu'i gzugs kyis kyang 'tshe bar byed pa'o/_/khrag gis rab tu bkang ba yi/_/mi yi pags pa thogs nas su/_/zhes bya ba ni sems can rnams kyi khrag 'jib cing /_mi yi pags pa gon 
[\[39a.4\]]pa'o/_/mi bzad dug gis kun bskus te/_/phyogs phyogs dag tu rnam par rgyug_/ces bya ba ni bsam pa dang /_mthong ba dang reg pa dang /_mche ba'i dug rnams kyis bskus pa/_stong gsum gyi stong gi 'jig rten gyi khams su rgyug pa'o/_/grong khyer rnams kyi sgo rnams dang /_/khyim 
[\[39a.5\]]nas khyim du rnam par 'thor/_/zhes bya ba ni sems can rnams la glags tshol ba/_grong dang grong khyer grong rdal du rgyu ba'o/_/rlung dang bad kan mkhris pa rnams/_/phyogs bzhi dag tu 'khrug par byed/_/ces bya ba ni 'byung po rnams kyi mdze dang phol mig la sogs pa nad bzhi brgya 
[\[39a.6\]]rtsa bzhi gtong ba'i bdag po'o/_/de ltar de na rgyal po yi/_/gdon dang 'jigs pa chen po rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni rgyal po chen po bzhis kyang 'byung po'i gdon de rnams 'dul ba'o/_/lhags pa de dag thams cad kyang /_/rig sngags zhags pas rab tu bsdams/_/zhes bya ba ni 
[\[39a.7\]]stong chen mo'i rig sngags kyis/_'byung po rnams rang dbang med par bkug pa'o/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpa' khyod la/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la/_/zhes bya ba ni ma rig pa la sogs pa nyon mongs pa 'dul ba la dpa' ba'o/_/skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum cu rtsa 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]gnyis dang /_dpe byad bzang pos sku la brgyan pa/_'jig rten gyi khams kyi sems can thams cad la mnyam pa nyid du byed pa'o/_/thal mo sbyar nas phyag 'tshal lo/_/chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po rnams kyis kyang gus par phyag 'tshal te/_stong gsum gyi 
[\[39b.2\]]chos kyi rgyal srid mdzad pa'o/_/grong khyer grong dang yul 'khor dang /_/pho brang 'khor du rgyu ba dang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po rgyu ba'i dus tshod ni snga dro shar gyi phyogs nas rgyu/_nyi ma phyed na lho nas rgyu/_nyi ma smyur nas nub kyi phyogs su rgyu/_nyi ma smyur nas ma nub bar du byang gi phyogs nas rgyu/_
[\[39b.3\]]nyi ma nub nas sa sros bar du shar lho mtshams su rgyu/_srod 'khor nam phyed bar du srin po'i phyogs nas rgyu/_nam phyed skya [({]rings [,]ring [},]rengs [)]tshun chad nub byang phyogs nas rgyu/_skya [({]rings [,]ring [},]rengs [)]shar nas nam langs tshun chad byang shar mtshams nas rgyu/_de dag dus rgyu ba ni/_mchod phyir lhags kyang 'tshe 
[\[39b.4\]]ba mod/_/tshes grangs dag la rgyu ba rnams/_/de dag mi la 'tshe bar byed/_/tshes gcig dang ni bcu gcig la shar gyi phyogs nas chu tshod bzhi'i bar du rgyu/_tshes gnyis dang bcu gsum la lho'i phyogs nas chu tshod brgyad rgyu/_tshes gsum dang bcu gnyis la chu tshod bcu'i bar du nub 
[\[39b.5\]]kyi phyogs nas rgyu/_srod 'khor tsam tshes bzhi dang bcu bzhi la nam phyed nas chu tshod drug byang gi phyogs nas rgyu/_tshes lnga dang bcu drug la ni nyi ma dros nas shar lho mtshams nas chu tshod bdun rgyu'o/_/tshes drug dang dgu la chu tshod sum cu tham pa lho nub mtshams nas snga dro nas rgyu'o/_/
[\[39b.6\]]tshes bdun dang bcu tham pa'i dus su nub byang mtshams nas snga dro nas chu tshod bco lnga la rgyu'o/_/tshes brgyad dang gnam stong la byang shar nas snga dro nas chu tshod bcu gnyis bar du rgyu'o/_/de dag ni 'byung po rgyu ba'i dus bstan pa'o/_/gnod sbyin ma rungs mdangs 'phrog pa/_/snying rje med cing 
[\[39b.7\]]khrag 'thung ba/_/zhes bya ba ni sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed cing srog 'phrog pa ste/_'byung po de rnams sha dang khrag la dga' ba'o/_lus chen 'jigs pa chen po ste/_/stobs po che la sgra yang che/_/zhes bya ba ni lus ni ri rab dang mnyam pa/_stobs kyis ni ri rab bsgul ba/_/
[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]skad kyang drag po'i tshul du snang ba'o/_/mgrin bcu po la mig stong po/_/gdon chen mig ni dmar po rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni mgo re re la mig brgya brgya yod pa ste/_mig de yang dmar por snang zhing 'jigs su rung ba'o/_/'khor ni mang po rnams btang ste/_/lag na 
[\[40a.2\]]mtshon thogs rab tu 'jigs/_/zhes bya ba ni gnod sbyin ma rungs pa mang por snang zhing /_ral gri la sogs pa'i mtshon cha mang po thogs pa'o/_/sbrul gyi pags pa thogs pa dang /_/lag bzhi lag na sgron me thogs/_/zhes bya ba ni dug sbrul gyi pags pa gon pa/_mi gsal 
[\[40a.3\]]ba gsal bar byed pa kha nas me 'bar zhing 'jigs par byed pa'o/_/lag na mtshon dang dbyig pa thogs/_/rdo rje'i mdung rtse lag na thogs/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po ma rungs pa rnams kyang /_lag na mtshon sna tshogs pa thogs shing 'jigs su rung ba'o/_/rab tu drag pa'i dmag 
[\[40a.4\]]brgal nas/_/phyogs bcu kun du skrag par byed/_/ces bya ba ni dpung mang po dang ldan zhing phyogs bcu dag tu 'khod pa ste/_de dag gis kyang sems can rnams la 'jigs pa dang skrag par byed pa'o/_/sdang ba'i gnas na 'khod pa yi/_/gsang ba po ni dad kun yang /_/zhes 
[\[40a.5\]]bya ba ni sems can rnams la sdang ba/_rgya cher gnod pa skyel ba'o/_/mi yi 'jig rten 'di dag tu/_/skyes pa bud med gzhan yang 'dzin/_/zhes bya ba ni stong gsum gyi mi rnams la 'byung po de rnams kyis 'tshe bar byas pa'o/_/sha khrag dron mor bcas pa la/_/rnam par 
[\[40a.6\]]'khrul zhing 'dod pa'i gzugs/_/zhes bya ba ni mi rnams kyi srog gcod cing sha dang khrag la dga' ba'o/_/seng ge dang ni stag dang rta/_/ma he ba glang bong bu rnga/_/zhes bya ba ni gzugs mi gcig pa byol song rnams kyi gzugs su sprul pa'o/_/glang po che yi gzugs 'dra 
[\[40a.7\]]dang /_/dom dang gzig dang spyang ki dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de dang der ldan pa'i gzugs su snang zhing /_sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed pa'o/_/khyi dang wa dang ra dag dang /_/byi la byi ba spre'u'i gzugs/_/zhes bya ba ni gnod pa skyel ba'i dus su gcan gzan gyi gzugs 
[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]su sprul pa'o/_/bse dang phag dang sre mong dang /_/nya dang rus sbal dag gi gzugs/_/zhes bya ba ni gzugs kyang sna tshogs su snang ba/_rmi lam yang de bzhin du snang ba'o/_/kha cig dag ni sram gyi gzugs/_/khwa dang 'ug pa khu byug dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de lta bu'i gzugs su snang 
[\[40b.2\]]zhing gnod pa cher skyel ba'o/_/bya rgod khra dang nya mid bcas/_/rma bya ngang pa thi ba dang /_/zhes bya ba ni de'i gzugs su ston cing /_skad mi gcig pa mang po 'don pa'o/_/khrung khrung dang ni bya rnams dang /_/kha cig bya gag gzugs rnams su/_/zhes bya ba ni 'jigs su rung ba 
[\[40b.3\]]ma rungs pa rnams yin te/_ltas dang rmi lam ngan par byed pa'o/_/gnod sbyin rnams ni mi yis mthong /_/'dab [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]bya yi tshul gyis kyang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'jigs su rung ba'i gzugs ston pa/_sems can rnams la skrag par byed pa'o/_/skye bo mang po skrag byed cing /_/zhes bya 
[\[40b.4\]]ba ni skyes pa bud med byis pa rnams la skrag par byed pa'o/_/kha cig mi yi mgo can la/_/lus ni bya gag bong bu 'dra/_/zhes bya ba ni 'jigs su rung ba'i 'byung po rnams kyang /_mi'i mgo can la byol song gi lus so/_/kha cig mtshon gyis rab tu 'tshe/_/lus dang bral ba nyid du 
[\[40b.5\]]snang /_/zhes bya ba ni sna tshogs mtshon gyis 'tshe zhing /_khrag khrug la dga' ba'o/_/rnam par 'khrugs shing 'dod la mos/_/rgyu ma'i phreng bas rab tu dkris/_/zhes bya ba ni 'dod pa'i yon tan lnga la dga' zhing sems 'khrugs par byed pa ste/_yan lag mi'i rgyu ma'i phreng 
[\[40b.6\]]bas brgyan pa'o/_/rtse gsum mdung gis rab 'debs shing /_/srog chags rnams la gnod par byed/_/ces bya ba ni 'jigs su rung ba'i 'byung po de dag kyang snying rje dang bral ba'o/_/mi bzad pa yi dbugs 'byin cing /_/skye dgu 'di dag skrag par byed/_/ces bya ba ni ske ba che 
[\[40b.7\]]zhing skad che ba ste/_sgra des sems can rnams skrag pa'o/_/srog chags rgyud rnams ji snyed par/_/sna tshogs kyi ni gzugs su snang /_/zhes bya ba ni nya la sogs pa'i sems can gyi gzugs su snang zhing /_gzugs mi gcig pa mang po snang ba'o/_/kha cig dag ni ri yang thogs/_/
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]la la ral gri 'khor lo thogs/_/zhes bya ba ni stobs dang ldan zhing ri rab theg par nus pa/_mtshon rnon pos 'tshe bar byed pa'o/_/grangs ni brgya phrag stong rnams te/_/dbyug pas bsdigs shing kha drag pa/_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po rnams sde chen por gnas shing 
[\[41a.2\]]sems can rnams la chad pa chen po gcod pa'o/_/mig chung ba la bzhin mi sdug_/srin po'i so ni rno ba dang /_/zhes bya ba ni bzhin mi sdug pa kha dang so rnams yo ba'o/_/sna dang rna ba bcad pa dang /_/lce bcad gdong ni gnyer ma can/_/zhes bya ba ni gzugs ngan par snang zhing /_dbang 
[\[41a.3\]]po rnams ma tshang ba'o/_/rkang lag dag ni gtubs pa dang /_/mgo bo bcad pa'i srin po rnams/_/zhes bya ba ni mgo bo med par brang nas gdong pa snang bas te/_tshigs rnams la yang gdong pa snang ba'o/_/skabs ni rab tu tshol bar byed/_/sdig pa can rnams gzi brjid 'phrog_/
[\[41a.4\]]ces bya ba ni sems can rnams la glags tshol zhing /_sdig pa'i sems dang ldan pa/_sems can gyi dpal 'phrog pa'o/_/mi yi lus rnams dag la ni/_/rab tu phra ba'i gdon du snang /_/zhes bya ba ni mi'i lus la zhugs nas mi dga' ba'i sems dang /_lceb pa'i sems 
[\[41a.5\]]dang ldan par byed pa'o/_/spu yi bar dang nad rnams dang /_/de bzhin rma yi kha rnams su/_/zhes bya ba ni ba spu'i bar du 'dzin cing /_rma med pa rma yod par byas te/_rma'i nang rul bar byas nas/_phyi subs nas [(]nang [,]nad [)]na tsha chen po dang ldan par byed do/_/lhags pa de dag thams cad 
[\[41a.6\]]kyang /_/rig sngags zhags pas rab tu drangs/_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags kyi dmod mo'i zhags pas ma rungs pa'i 'byung po rnams bcings pa'o/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu dpa' khyod la/_/phyag 'tshal skyes bu mchog khyod la/_/zhes bya ba ni de dang der ldan pa'i 'byung po 
[\[41a.7\]]rnams kyis kyang /_yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la gus par phyag 'tshal ba'o/_/thal mo sbyar nas phyag 'tshal lo/_/chos rgyal khyod la phyag 'tshal lo/_/zhes bya ba ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams chos srid du mdzad cing /_stong gsum gyi chos 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]kyi rgyal por snang ba'o/_/ri yi rgyal po ri rab dang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'dod pa'i khams ni 'dod yon lnga la spyod pa bzhin/_'byung po rnams kyang 'dod pa'i yon tan lngas spyod do/_/ji ltar spyod ce na/_gzugs mthong bas mdangs 'phrog pa dang /_sgra snyan po thos nas 'dzin pa dang /_dri zhim po tshor nas 'dzin pa 
[\[41b.2\]]dang /_ro zhim po myangs nas 'dzin pa dang /_lus reg pas 'dzin pa dang /_de dag kyang gang na gnas she na/_ri rab khor yug la gnas nas 'dzin pa'o/_/de bzhin kho ra khor yug dang /_/bya rgod phung po gshol mda' 'dzin/_/zhes bya ba ni dbus kyi ri bo mchog rab nas/_phyi'i kho ra khor 
[\[41b.3\]]yug la thug pa'i ri bo chen po la gnas pa'i 'byung po rnams so/_/de bzhin du bya rgod phung po dang /_ri bdun la sogs pa'o/_/spos ngad ldan dang gangs kyi ri/_/sna tshogs sde dang paN+Da pA/_/zhes bya ba ni de yang 'dzam bu'i gling na gnas pa'i ri rnams so/_/na la ngA yi ri 
[\[41b.4\]]gnyis dang /_/dpal gyi ri yi rtse mo ni/_/zhes bya ba ni 'dzam bu'i gling gi lho phyogs na gnas pa'o/_/gang dag shin tu mthor 'phags dang /_/ri bo de dag kun la yang /_/zhes bya ba ni lha dang lha ma yin rnams 'tshogs shing /_'byung po'i tshogs rnams 'dul ba'o/_/lha 
[\[41b.5\]]dang drang srong gang gnas pa/_/de dag thams cad mi dga' zhing /_/zhes bya ba ni stong chen po rab tu 'joms ma'i gzi brjid kyis/_ri rab de rnams na gnas pa'i 'byung po rnams yid mi dga' ba'o/_/'jigs pa'i yid kyis mya ngan byed/_/bye ba stong gi lha dag dang /_/bye ba phrag brgya'i 
[\[41b.6\]]lha rnams dang /_/zhes bya ba ni rig sngags kyi gzi brjid kyis/_'dar zhing kyi hud ces sgra 'byin pa'o/_/lha yi bu mo skal chen dag_/thal mo rab tu sbyor bar byed/_/ces bya ba ni rig sngags kyi dmod mos 'jigs pas gus par sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal lo/_/thags [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]ris

[\[42a.1\]]yin gyi grangs/_'byung po la gtogs pa rnams so/_/de dag bu mo 'phrul ldan pa/_/mang po kun kyang thal mo sbyar/_/zhes bya ba ni lha ma yin gyi dbang po'i bu mo la sogs pa yang /_rig sngags kyi gzi brjid kyis/_sangs rgyas kyi spyan sngar 'khod pa'o/_/rab tu

[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]bya ba ni yul ma ga d+hA na gnas shing /_stong gsum rgyu ba'o/_/bzang po'i yul na kha spu can/_/gyad kyi yul na grags pa 'dzin/_/zhes bya ba ni de yang 'dzam bu'i gling gi nub phyogs su gnas shing /_sems can rnams la 'tshe bar byed pa'o/_/lnga 'dzin pa na skrag byed pa/_/rta

[\[45a.5\]]de lta'i rab tu dga' ba yis/_/bde zhing tshim pa'i yid du gyur/_/zhes bya ba ni yang dag pa'i ye shes dang ldan zhing bde bar gnas pa'o/_/de na bdag ni rgun skyems dang /_/'dod pa dag gis rab myos pas/_/zhes bya ba ni de yang 'byung po la sogs pa'i 'dod pa'i yon

[\[45b.1\]]'phrog pa'o/_/skar ma dag dang nyi zla dang /_/gtum pa'i gza' rnams gnod par bgyid/_/ces bya ba ni zla ba nyi ma mi snang bar byas shing /_bza' ba dang btung ba rnams 'phrog pa'o/_/lo thog sa bon 'bras bu dang /_/me tog sman dang zas skom dang /_/zhes bya ba ni 'byung po de

[\[45b.4\]]mi rnams kyi srog 'phrog pa'o/_/sbed cing stor bar bgyid pa dang /_/zlog par bgyid cing rgod par byed/_/ces bya ba ni 'byung po de rnams bzung nas/_zas dang skom yang mi 'ju zhing bzlog ste/_sbed pa ni nad sna tshogs gtong zhing sems yang stor bar byed pa'o/_/res 'ga'
